## Rookie of the Year Standings: 2016-17

#### Updated August 1, 2017

The player with the most Pro Points at the end of a tournament season who has not participated in a Pro Tour or World Championship tournament in any previous season is identified as the 2016-17 Rookie of the Year.

*Some rows on this leaderboard do not currently have a name associated with them. Information on this issue was explained in the [following article](http://magicorganizedplay.tumblr.com/post/159201598541/hey-everyone-we-processed-our-usual-weekly-update). If you believe you are affected by this, follow the instructions in that article regarding your Wizards Account as soon as possible. Thanks for your cooperation!*
